5.6.
An analogue of the Borel-Weil theorem

Recall the embedding

\mathchar 28947_{\mathchar 28949}\mathchar 12346\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\mathrel{\mathchar 300\mkern-3.0mu\mathchar 545}\mathbb{\mathchar 29008}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{V}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\delimiter 84054785

from Subsection
5.1
above. Consider the pull-back of the canonical line bundle
\mathcal{\mathchar 29007}_{\mathbb{\mathchar 29008}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{V}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\delimiter 84054785}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28721\delimiter 84054785
with respect to the embedding:

\mathcal{\mathchar 29004}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\mathchar 12346\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28947_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathchar 8707}\delimiter 67273472\mathcal{\mathchar 29007}_{\mathbb{\mathchar 29008}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{V}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\delimiter 84054785}\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 28721\delimiter 84054785\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 314

Recall the degenerate group
\textsc{N}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}
acting on
\textsc{V}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}
. By an argument similar to the proof of
[
FFFM19
, Theorem 8.1]
and from Corollary

, we deduce the following analogue of the Borel-Weil theorem in our context.

Theorem 5.11
.

For every
\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 29003}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
, we have
\mathrm{\mathchar 29000}^{\mathchar 28720}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\mathchar 24891\mathcal{\mathchar 29004}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 12839\delimiter 67273472\textsc{V}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\delimiter 84054785^{\mathchar 8707}
as
\textsc{N}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}
-modules.

Moreover, the following Borel-Weil type vanishing theorem holds true.

Theorem 5.12
.

\mathrm{\mathchar 29000}^{\mathchar 29037}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\mathchar 24891\mathcal{\mathchar 29004}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}\delimiter 84054785\mathchar 12349\mathchar 28720
for all
\mathchar 28949\mathchar 12850\mathchar 28675^{\mathchar 8235}
and all
\mathchar 29037\mathchar 12606\mathchar 28720
.

Proof.

By
[
Ful93
, Sec. 3.5]
, the theorem holds for any
\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}
in the relative interior of
\mathcal{\mathchar 29003}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
, since the line bundle
\mathcal{\mathchar 29004}_{\mathchar 28949}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}
is generated by its sections. By Theorem
3.2
(2), it follows that for any generic point
\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}^{\mathchar 560}\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 29003}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
, one has a flat family over
\mathbb{\mathchar 28993}^{\mathchar 28721}
with the generic fiber

and

.
∎

.

, the cone
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28724}\mathchar 12818\mathbb{\mathchar 29010}^{\mathcal{\mathchar 29008}}
is defined by the following equalities and inequalities:

Proof of Theorem
3.2
(1)

First notice that any Plücker coordinate
\mathchar 29016_{\mathchar 29002}
for
\mathchar 29002\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 29008}
can not vanish identically on any
\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}}
for
\mathbf{\mathchar 29028}\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 29003}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
. From Theorem
5.2
, for any point in
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
, the initial ideal of
\mathfrak{\mathchar 29001}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
associated to this point is a prime ideal, which contains no monomials, hence
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
is contained in the tropical symplectic flag variety
\mathrm{\mathchar 29012\mathchar 29042\mathchar 29039\mathchar 29040}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}\delimiter 84054785
. It remains to show that this cone is a maximal cone since the primeness follows from Theorem
5.2
. The idea of the proof is the same as
[
FFFM19
, Theorem 7.3]
.

Since the dimension of
\mathrm{\mathchar 29012\mathchar 29042\mathchar 29039\mathchar 29040}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}\delimiter 84054785
is
\mathchar 29038^{\mathchar 28722}
, the cone
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
is of maximal dimension. Assume there is a maximal cone
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathchar 560}\mathchar 12826\mathrm{\mathchar 29012\mathchar 29042\mathchar 29039\mathchar 29040}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}\delimiter 84054785
that contains
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
, then
\dim\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathchar 560}\mathchar 12349\mathchar 29038^{\mathchar 28722}
and
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathchar 560}
must be contained in the image of
\mathchar 29047
. Therefore according to the proof of Lemma
6.1
, any point
\mathchar 29043\mathchar 12346\mathchar 12349\delimiter 67273472\mathchar 29043_{\mathchar 29002}\delimiter 84054785_{\mathchar 29002\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 29008}}\mathchar 12850\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathchar 560}
must satisfy the linear equalities (i)-(iv) of Lemma
6.1
. We want to show that
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathchar 560}\mathchar 12818\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}
, so we are left with showing that the point
\mathchar 29043
satisfies the inequalities (v)-(viii) of Lemma
6.1
. We argue by showing that no point outside
\mathcal{\mathchar 28995}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}^{\mathchar 560}
lies in
\mathrm{\mathchar 29012\mathchar 29042\mathchar 29039\mathchar 29040}\delimiter 67273472\textsc{Sp}\mathcal{\mathchar 28998}_{\mathchar 28722\mathchar 29038}\delimiter 84054785
. This is accomplished by considering each of the four types of facets corresponding to the inequalities (v)-(viii) as follows:

(v)

We consider for
\mathchar 28721\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29033\mathchar 12820\mathchar 29038\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721
the Plücker relation:

\textsc{X}_{\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033}\textsc{X}_{\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 8235\textsc{X}_{\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28722}\textsc{X}_{\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28721}\mathchar 8704\textsc{X}_{\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8704\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28721}\textsc{X}_{\mathchar 28721\mathchar 24891\ldots\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 24891\mathchar 29033\mathchar 8235\mathchar 28722}\mathchar 314

From Lemma
